Document 001: 1-9月财政收支数据解读：9月财政收入同比转为微降

Source PDF: all report pdf/1-9月财政收支数据解读：9月财政收入同比转为微降.pdf
Report source: 国新证券股份
Date: 2026-11-01
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

市场研究部 2026年10月25日
事件
10 月 23 日，财政部发布，1-9 月，全国一般公共预算收入
172292亿元，同比增长 5.1%；全国一般公共预算支出 210199亿
元，同比增长 1%。
解读
一、1-9 月财政收入增速比 1-8 月小幅回落，9 月财政收入同
比转为微降。
1-9月，全国一般公共预算收入 172292亿元，同比增长 5.1%，
比 1-8月回落 0.6个百分点，比 2025年同期提高 4.6个百分点。
其中，9 月，全国一般公共预算收入 15659 亿元，同比下降
0.1%，比 8月下滑 4.8 个百分点，比 2025 年同期回落 2.7 个百分
点。
1-9月，全国税收收入同比增长 6.1%，比 1-8月回落 0.5个百
分点，比 2025年同期提高 5.4个百分点；非税收入同比增长 1.2%，
比 1-8月回落 0.3个百分点，比 2025年同期提高 1.6个百分点。
其中，9 月全国税收收入同比增长 0.2%，比 8 月回落 5.4 个
百分点，比 2025 年同期回落 8.5 个百分点；非税收入同比增长

5.7%，比 8 月提高 6.9 个百分点，比 2025 年同期提高 17.1 个百
分点。
分中央和地方看，1-9 月，中央一般公共预算收入同比增长
8.3%，比 1-8月回落 0.8个百分点；地方一般公共预算本级收入同
比增长 2.7%，比 1-8月回落 0.4个百分点。
9月，中央一般公共预算收入同比增长 0.2%，比 8月回落 9.1
个百分点；地方一般公共预算本级收入同比-0.34%，比 8 月回落
1.05个百分点。
二、9 月国内增值税、国内消费税同比增速比 8 月提高，企业
所得税、进口税收、个人所得税、证券交易印花税等税种增速比8
月回落，证券交易印花税同比转为下降。
1-9 月，国内增值税 55416 亿元，同比增长 6%，比 1-8 月提
高 0.1 个百分点。其中，9 月同比增长 7.3%， 比 8 月提高 3.3 个
百分点。
宏观研究
9月财政收入同比转为微降
——1-9月财政收支数据解读
市场表现截至 2026.8.31
资料来源：Wind,国新证券整理。
分析师：郝大明

登记编码：S1490514010002
邮箱：haodaming@crsec.com.cn
证券研究报告

### Page 2

国内消费税 12402 亿元，同比下降 4.1%，降幅比 1-8 月扩大
0.5个百分点。 其中，9月同比下降 8.5%，降幅比 8月收窄 5.5个
百分点。
企业所得税 34832 亿元，同比增长 7.1%，比 1-8 月回落 0.2
个百分点。 其中，9 月同比增长 0.4%， 比 8 月下滑 12.0 个百分
点。
个人所得税 13464亿元，同比增长 14.1%，比 1-8月回落 0.4
个百分点。 其中，9 月同比增长 10.5%， 比 8 月回落 1.3 个百分
点。
进口货物增值税、消费税 15171 亿元，同比增长 12.7%，比
1-8月提高 0.1 个百分点。其中，9月同比增长 12.9%， 比 8月回
落 2.0个百分点。
出口货物退增值税、消费税 18087亿元，同比增长 6.3%，比
1-8月提高 1.8个百分点。 其中，9月同比增长 29.0%， 比 8月提
高 30.7个百分点。
1-9 月，印花税 4121 亿元，同比增长 31.1%，比 1-8 月回落
4.5个百分点。其中，9月同比下降 11.7%， 比 8月下滑 26.8个百

分点。
1-9月，证券交易印花税 2391亿元，同比增长 65.1%，比 1-8
月回落 16.9 个百分点。 其中，9月同比下降 11.5%， 比 8月下滑
29.4个百分点。
三、1-9 月全国财政支出小幅增长，比 1-8 月小幅回落，9 月
同比微增。
1-9月，全国一般公共预算支出 210199亿元，同比增长 1%，
比 1-8月回落 0.2个百分点。
分中央和地方看，中央一般公共预算本级支出 32820 亿元，
同比增长 5.8%，比 1-8 月回落 0.2 个百分点；地方一般公共预算
支出 177379亿元，同比增长 0.2%，比 1-8月回落 0.1个百分点。
1-9月，财政支出同比增长的项目是，卫生健康支出同比增长
7.7%，比 1-8月回落 1.5个百分点，社会保障支出同比增长 5.2%，
比 1-8月回落 1.3个百分点，债务付息支出同比增长 4.7%，比 1-8
月回落 0.7 个百分点，科学技术支出同比增长 3.0%，比 1-8 月提
高 0.8 个百分点，教育支出同比增长 1.1%，比 1-8 月提高 0.4 个

百分点。
其余支出项目 1-9 月均同比下降，其中，农林水支出同比下降
5.1%，节能环保支出同比下降 12.5%，文化旅游支出同比下降
6.0%，交通运输支出同比下降 4.1%，城乡社区事务支出同比下降
1.5%。

### Page 3

四、1-9 月政府性基金预算收入及国有土地使用权出让收入无
同比大幅下降，9月同比降幅比8 月小幅收窄。
1-9 月，全国政府性基金预算收入 25010 亿元，同比下降
18.6%，降幅比 1-8 月收窄 0.4 个百分点。分中央和地方看，中央
政府性基金预算收入 3682 亿元，同比增长 12.4%，比 1-8 月提高
0.2个百分点；地方政府性基金预算本级收入 21328亿元，同比下
降 22.3%，降幅比 1-8月收窄 0.6个百分点，其中，国有土地使用
权出让收入 16087亿元，同比下降 27.9%，降幅比 1-8月收窄 0.7
个百分点。
9月，全国政府性基金预算收入同比-15.9%，降幅比 8月扩大
12.1个百分点；分中央和地方看，中央政府性基金预算收入同比增
长 13.7%，比 8 月回落 40.6 个百分点，地方政府性基金预算本级
收入同比-18.4%，降幅比 8月扩大 8.1个百分点，其中，国有土地
使用权出让收入同比-23.2%，降幅比 8月扩大 10.6个百分点。

1-9 月，全国政府性基金预算支出 51948 亿元，同比下降
17.0%，降幅比 1-8 月扩大 0.6 个百分点。分中央和地方看，中央
政府性基金预算本级支出 3175亿元，同比下降 58.3%，降幅比 1-
8月收窄 3.5个百分点；地方政府性基金预算支出 48773亿元，同
比下降 11.3%，降幅比 1-8 月扩大 1.8 个百分点，其中，国有土地
使用权出让收入相关支出 21786 亿元，同比下降 18.5%，降幅比
1-8月扩大 1.1个百分点。
9月，全国政府性基金预算支出 62754亿元，同比下降 16.2%，
降幅比 8月收窄 5.2个百分点。分中央和地方看，中央政府性基金
预算本级支出 3692亿元，同比下降 54.4%，降幅比 8月扩大 45.6
个百分点；地方政府性基金预算支出 59062 亿元，同比下降
11.6%，降幅比 8 月扩大 10.6 个百分点，其中，国有土地使用权
出让收入相关支出 25565 亿元，同比下降 18.5%，降幅比 8 月收
窄 8.3个百分点。

五、预测10月财政收入同比增速小幅提高。
9月国内增值税、国内消费税同比增速比 8月提高，企业所得
税、进口税收、个人所得税、证券交易印花税等税种增速比 8月回
落，证券交易印花税同比转为下降。预测 10 月财政收入同比增速
小幅提高。
投资建议
1、9月国内增值税、国内消费税同比增速比 8月提高。
2、9月证券交易印花税同比转降。
3、9月出口退税同比大幅增加。
4、9月企业所得税、个人所得税同比增速回落。

风险提示
1、地缘政治恶化超预期；
2、欧美经济衰退超预期。

制造业PMI 非制造业PMI
10月份制造业PMI季节性回落至收缩区间（49.3）。分项来看，价格分项高位回落但仍维持扩
张，购进和出厂价格分别为  56.8（-4.0）和51.7（-2.3）  ，二者差缩至  5.1pcts  ，中下游利润挤
压边际缓解。生产指数  49.8（-1.9）  、新订单  49.4（-1.1）  ，产需缺口由上月1.2pcts收窄
至  0.4pcts  ，两者均落入收缩区间，指向企业根据订单变化主动下调产出节奏。新出口订单回落

至  48.7（-1.3）  ，不过仍高于过去五年同期水平，显示外需支撑边际减弱但仍具有相对韧性。原
材料库存  48.4（+0.2）  、产成品库存  47.5（-0.1）  ，叠加采购量  48.9  （-2.1）与进口  47.0（-
2.2）  回落，反映订单走弱背景下企业“以销定产”。 高技术  51.3  与装备  50.6  维持扩张，对冲总
量回落，而消费品  48.8  、高耗能  47.3  拖累更加明显，“K型分化”扩大。大型企业  50.2  相对稳
定，中小企业收缩进一步深化，分化加剧。非制造业商务活动指数  49.7（-0.5）  ，服务业  50.0
（-0.2）  ，建筑业  48.0（-2.3）未能延续9月的回升态势，“银十”未形成有效托底，建筑开工
进度偏慢。总体来看，10月回落幅度与历史季节性大致相当，短期扰动（长假与前期备货透支）
叠加需求接续不足是主因，四季度修复仍依赖财政资金加快转化为实物工作量与内需改善落地。

王文雯 wenwen.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

瑞昱 (2379 TT) 
財報與展望均優於預期

永豐觀點 
目前股價持穩於前季法說時點，而公司在財報與展望(包括毛利率、營業費用控管)
似乎均有好轉現象，尤其毛利率似乎出現谷底回溫 (優於研究部原預期的內外承
壓)，又公司在網通/車載等業務持續進展，股價應有上升空間。 
 投資評價與建議 
中立調升至買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控
管，故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66
元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶片
大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力 (或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

ESG 評析 
瑞昱企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

瑞昱為網通晶片大廠 ：瑞昱為全球網通晶片大廠，若以公司營收排名為全球第八大
IC 設計廠商。瑞昱產品線含括 Ethernet、WiFi、藍芽、Switch 與 TV SoC 。網通
領域的參與廠商為博通、Marvell、高通與聯發科等，TV SoC 的參與廠商則包括聯
發科、聯詠等。 
3Q26 獲利優於預期：瑞昱 3Q26 營收 377.32 億元(+0.5%QoQ，+27.9% YoY)，
毛利率 47.4%，稅後獲利 41.26 億元(+8%QoQ，+20%YoY)，稅後 EPS 8 元，獲
利優於預期，主要是營收、毛利率與營業費用控管均較預期佳。瑞昱3Q26 財報細
項要點如下：(1) 營收季增 0.5%，優於研究部原預期的季減 3%，主要是網通業務
成長驅使，以及部分產品價格調漲，從而抵銷筆電/TV/遊戲機需求減緩的衝擊。若
究終端應用分拆，PC 因終端市場需求疲軟，瑞昱 3Q26 PC 比重由前季的 33%降
至 30%(3Q26 PC: 非 PC 比重為 30%:70%。(2) 毛利率由 2Q26 的 47%微幅增至
47.4%(優於研究部原預期將受製造成本上升而使毛利率承壓)，主要是產品結構變
化(高階產品比重增加 )，以及產品因應成本上揚而進行轉嫁。 (3) 營業費用季減
0.6%，反映公司在投資研發的同時，仍對營業費用謹慎控管。(4) 業外收益僅 6.06
億元(季減 3900 萬元)， 主要是包含金融資產評價損失 。(5) 瑞昱庫存天數雖由2Q26
的 107 天增至 3Q26 的 136 天，不過主要是供應鏈持續有不確定性，公司為確保
客戶供貨的連貫性，故多建置庫存因應。 
4Q26 營收降幅不會如同過往第四季顯著：瑞昱認為(1) 4Q26 營運審慎穩健，雖
第四季通常為營運淡季， 但隨產品結構往高階挪移， 又價格為反映製造成本上升而
仍有調漲事宜，故營收降幅較為平緩，其中PC 需求仍相對疲弱。(3) 2H26 毛利率
將逐步回溫。整體而言，研究處預估瑞昱 4Q26 營收 348.5 億元(-7.6%QoQ，
+32.6%YoY)，毛利率隨產品結構變化與轉嫁而略增至 47.6%，稅後獲利 35.34 億
元(-14%QoQ，+33%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.86 元。

### Page 3

3

2027 年營運應仍能維持成長力道：研究部認為台系 IC 設計族群今明年將持續遭
遇內外交雜的挑戰(內有製造成本攀升、外有消費電子需求疲軟)，不過瑞昱身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品優化與議價能力均相對同業更具競爭力，使得營運表現
應能優於產業狀態(其實從瑞昱財報亦能得證，公司即使 30%營收與 PC 相關，但
其 3Q26 獲利數字與 4Q26 展望均仍穩健)。研究部認為瑞昱動能來自下述因子驅
動：(1) 網通隨電信業者對 25GPON 架構進行結構性測試、乙太網路交換器受益
於硬體升級、消費者與中小企業路由器往Ｍesh/三頻配置/WiFi7 升級，並且企業
級網路管理需求與管理型交換器持續普及中，均將推升瑞昱網通業務長期穩健成
長。(2) WiFi7 滲透率提升。隨瑞昱客戶的新開案均基於 WiFi7 作設計，瑞昱預期
WiFi7 滲透率將由 2026 年的 30%攀升至 40%。WiFi 聯盟計畫 WiFi8 將於 06/2027
完成認證測試，並將於 09/2027 前正式啟動 WiFi8 認證，瑞昱預期 2H27 公司
WiFI8 產品可望步入量產，以利客戶搭載 WiFi8 產品更廣泛於 2028 年上市。(3) 
光通訊專注 112G/224G SerDes 開發(4nm 製程)，100G 光模組控制晶片將以雲
端、資料中心與電信網路為主要著墨項目，明年量產規模提升，並且 400G/800G 
PAM4 DSP預計 2H27 推出。(4) 電動車成長持續帶動瑞昱車載乙太網路進展，瑞
昱預期新車搭載乙太網路的滲透率已超過 50%。(5) 中長期邊緣 AI 業務(包括車載
/機器人/IOT)與高效能運算 (比如瑞昱導入乙太網路 /交換器/xPON/SSD/IO 橋接
器/USB hub/光通訊)，目標 2030 年營收比重達 30%。 
中立轉買進：由於財報與展望均優於預期，毛利率谷底回溫，營業費用謹慎控管，
故研究部上修獲利預估值，預估瑞昱2026 年營收 1466 億元(+19%YoY)，毛利率
47.91%，稅後獲利 157.9 億元(+7%YoY)，稅後 EPS 30.64 元(原預估 27.66 元)，
並預估 2027 年營收 1594 億元(+9%YoY)，毛利率 48.37%，稅後獲利 181.4 億
元(+15%YoY)，稅後 EPS 35.19 元(原預估 29.15 元)。研究部認為瑞昱(1) 身為晶
片大廠，在資源調度、產品完整度、晶片優化或議價能力，相對小廠更具競爭力(或
許也有機會提高市場份額)，此似乎也體現於 3Q26 財報與 4Q26 展望中，(2) 中
長期受會網通/車載/伺服器/邊緣 AI 裝置涉足而帶動營運成長，故將評等由中立調
升至買進建議，目標價 915 元(以 26X2027 年 EPS 計算)。

### Page 1

Poya  (5904.TWO)
Strong 3Q as Expected; Tougher Comparisons Ahead
CITI'S TAKE 
Poya’s 3Q26 net profit +26% y/y came largely in-line with our forecast, and 
5% ahead of BBG consensus. SSSG of 7.0%, higher GPM, opex savings and 
stronger op leverage lifted OPM to 17.5%, the key drivers to strong 
profitability. Looking into 4Q, tougher comp should moderate sales and 
earnings growth despite fast store additions and solid operation execution. 
We maintain Neutral as the strong share price rally YTD appears to reflect 
most positives from robust SSSG and margin expansion. A higher comp 
base and potentially intensifying competition limit the room for further re-
rating. 
3Q26 broadly in-line; solid margins boosted by strong op leverage — Poya 
reported 3Q26 net profits of NT$1.0bn +26% y/y, largely in-line with our est and 5% 
above BBG’s est. 3Q sales of NT$7.4bn +15% y/y were supported by solid SSSG 
+7.0% y/y with ticket size +4.9% and traffic +2.0%. Higher GPM, strong opex 
savings and op leverage lifted OPM to 17.5%, topping our/BBG est of 17.1%/16.6%. 
T otal store count reached 495 at end-3Q26 (+25 net increase in 9M26). 
4Q outlook: faster store expansion meets a higher comp base — We expect 30 net 
store additions in 4Q26E vs. 35 stores prior, bringing the year-end store count to 
525 and supporting continued sales growth. Demand for beauty-related products 
remains healthy. However, the elevated comparison base from 4Q26E onward 
should moderate SSSG and overall sales and earnings growth, despite the 
contribution from new stores. We believe this potential growth deceleration could 
temper investor enthusiasm and trigger profit-taking pressure.
Implications: Slightly positive reaction expected; maintain Neutral — We keep our 
earnings estimates for 26-28E largely unchanged in view of the in-line 3Q results. 
We expect a slightly positive market reaction to the results given the healthy margin 
expansion. However, strong sales had likely raised expectations for 3Q profitability, 
limiting the scope for a more meaningful positive surprise. Our Neutral thesis 
remains unchanged – we believe the strong share price rally has priced in much of 
the benefit from robust SSSG and margin improvement. Looking ahead, tougher 
comp could constrain growth, while potentially intensifying competition, including 
Cosmed’s introduction of Olive Young products, could challenge Poya’s beauty-led 
momentum. We therefore see limited room for further re-rating despite solid 
operating execution.

Angela Hsu AC

Poya
Company description
Poya is the largest retailer of fast-moving consumer goods in Taiwan, focusing
on low-priced cosmetics, accessories, clothing and food products for females
aged 15-49. Poya had 470 stores in Taiwan at the end of 2025 and believes
that the market can accommodate up to 700-800 stores before reaching
saturation.
Investment strategy
We have a Neutral rating on Poya. We think YTD rally has baked in most

positives, including strong SSSG and solid margin expansion. While
fundamentals remain solid, tougher comparisons from 4Q26E could
moderate this year's 6-7% SSSG, lowering operating leverage and capping
further earnings upside. Meanwhile, Cosmed's expanding rollout of Olive
Young brands is intensifying competition for beauty spending. Although
Poya's SSSG has remained resilient, braoder distribution of these brands
could divert customer traffic and increase promotional pressure. We continue

to recognize Poya's execution in store expansion and ongoing efficiency
improvements. However, with these strengths largely reflected in the share
price and growth momentum facing a higher hurdle from 4Q26E onward, we
see a more balanced risk/reward.
Valuation
We derive our 12-month target price of NT$70 for Poya from a DCF valuation
method. Our assumptions include a terminal growth rate of 4.5% and WACC
of 10.1% (beta of 1.05; risk-free rate of 4%; risk premium of 7.0%; cost of equity

of 11.3%). Our target price implies a P/E of 18x on our NTM EPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean the shares fail to achieve our target price
include: intense price competition among online retailers, slower store traffic,
margin dilution due to aggressive store expansion, and a soft macro
environment.
Key upside risks that could mean the shares fail to achieve our target price
include: easing price competition among online retailers, stronger store

traffic, margin expansion thanks to strong cost control, and a stronger macro
environment.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 1

3Q recap - strong op leverage offset GPM pressure — 3Q26 net profits (+125% y/y) 
topped our/BBG estimates 35%/28% respectively owing mostly to stronger sales 
and larger degree of op leverage. Sales reached a record NT$32.4bn (+24% q/q and 
+83% y/y), 23%/12% above Citi/BBG estimate, which can be attributable to strong 
demand in edge AI, semi equipment and smart healthcare applications. GPM fell to 
36.7% (-1ppt q/q and -2.2ppt y/y) below Citi/BBG estimates of 38.0%/37.6%, 
reflecting higher component prices. Nevertheless, OPM expanded to 20.9%, 
underpinned by strong op leverage on higher sales scale. OP profits +151% y/y beat 
Citi/BBG forecast 33%/21%, the key driver to the earnings beat.    
Implications — Strong order intake is encouraging with 3Q BB ratio reaching 1.6x 
and NA particularly robust at 2.1x. However, we believe the stock's rich valuation 
limits further re-rating potential, particularly as a higher comparison base could 
moderate growth in 2027E. Continued GPM compression also reinforces our 
concerns over material/component inflation and the timing of price increases to 
offset higher costs. Advantech will host 3Q26 results call on Nov 4th. We will revisit 
our numbers and share key takeaways following management's updates.

Advantech
Valuation
Our target price of NT$670 is based on 35x 2H26-1H27E EPS, at the high-cycle of the stock's valuation range between 20-35x
since 2020, reflecting solid BB ratio, improving margin profile and ROE.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause Advantech’s shares to trade above our target price include: 1) better-than-expected
marcoeconomic outlook; and 2) better-than-expected demand and margin profile. Key downside risks would include: 1)

slower-than-expected demand recovery, especially the China market; and 2) further deterioration of demand in US and EU
markets.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Idea
Further upside surprise: In our recent CPO report, we raised WIN Semi's revenue
forecasts  to reflect stronger demand for satellite communications RF, driven by
major customer SpaceX (SPCX; covered by Adam Jonas). However, we
underestimated the favorable pricing and margin, as evidenced by the 28 Oct   3Q26
results. Reflecting the much higher gross margin, we raise our 2026, 2027 and 2028
EPS by 35%, 28% and 17%, respectively, and raise our price target 17% to  NT$700  to

capture the long-term upside from both new businesses - space communications
and AI optical networking laser.
3Q26 margin beat: Core margin reached 40.7% in 3Q26, well above our original
forecast of 29%. The company indicated space communications revenue already
represents 40% of its infrastructure business, accounting for 35-40% of total
revenue. This implies the space-related revenue is tracking at c.15% of total revenue
in 3Q. Despite a relatively  small portion of revenue, we estimate the business could

lift 3Q26 margin by 5% (half of 3Q26 increase), implying the space communications
margin could be >60%, well above our and Street expectations. V3 satellite
development will be a key growth driver into 2027. See Exhibit 1  for our analysis.
4Q26 revenue guidance looks solid: Smartphone and WiFi revenue remain stable
despite weak China smartphone demand: Win Semi now has higher exposure to
Apple iPhone through high-band PA (Broadcom) and WiFi (Murata). As such,

revenue can still grow single digit QoQ in 4Q26, despite recent weakness in China
smartphone demand. Please see Exhibit 3  and Exhibit 4    for  our earnings review.
Upgrade to OW: The stock trades at 25x our new 2028 EPS. Given the significant
revenue contribution from space communications and AI lasers, we think our
implied target multiple of 29x 2028 EPS should be justified. Win Semi historically
traded at 40x PE during the smartphone 3D sensing product cycle.

2
Surprising Margin Expansion from Optical/Space Businesses
WIN Semi   could benefit from PD growth and potential LD outsourcing
by late 2027
In our previous WIN Semi report, we argued that Lumentum (LITE.O, covered by Meta
Marshall) was unlikely to outsource CW lasers or EML production to WIN Semi, a key
pillar of our earlier UW rating. However, our recent  checks with WIN Semi management
suggest that WIN Semi and its major customer, we assume it's Broadcom (AVGO, covered

by Joseph Moore), have secured sufficient InP substrate supply for WIN Semi's PD
production, potentially supporting substantial growth for the PD (photo diode) business
in 2H26. We estimate PD could account for >10% of WIN Semi's total revenue. Our recent
checks from the supply chain also suggest that WIN Semi's largest PD customer  could
outsource LD (laser diode) production to WIN Semi.
We also see the potential for even stronger revenue growth from WIN Semi's aerospace

business into 2027, especially from the ground station and satellite business. With
SpaceX's  launch of Starship and Starlink V3 satellite, we expect WIN Semi's content value
per satellite   to increase significantly from Starlink v2 to v3, with potential upside from
future DTD (direct to device) satellites. The increase in content value comes not only from
higher RF chips volumes, but also from the migration from GaAs to GaN. Based on our
industry checks, the ASP for 4-inch GaN could be US$9k, compared with ~US$2.5-3k for 6-

inch GaAs. We therefore upgrade our rating from EW to OW with the strong gross margin
performance, which we believe to be >60%.
Where we could be wrong with our OW call: We think the largest near-term swing factor
for WIN Semi is the aerospace business, which is relatively difficult to track given the
inherent risk of delays or failures in satellite launches. However, on September 28, 2026,
SpaceX successfully launched the Starship with Starlink V3 satellite. If SpaceX decides to

speed up the satellite constellation, there also could be upside to our forecasts.
All of our satellite launch numbers are based on Adam Jonas's SpaceX forecasts. Please
see his latest report: SpaceX: SpaceX/Tesla and SI's Next Act (23 Oct 2026)

3Q26 earnings and 4Q26 guidance review - margin and earnings beat on

strong optical and aerospace demand
WIN Semi delivered a solid 3Q26 performance, with margins and earnings exceeding
expectations, supported by robust demand across its optical and aerospace businesses.
The gross margin beat MSe and consensus by 7-8ppts.  Strength in optical applications,
driven by continued investment in AI-related optical connectivity and high-speed data
transmission, alongside resilient aerospace demand, supported a favorable product mix

and improved manufacturing efficiency.
The higher contribution from these relatively high-value-added segments lifted gross
margins above expectations, driving positive operating leverage and stronger-than-
anticipated earnings. Looking ahead, we expect sustained momentum in optical
communications and aerospace applications to remain key drivers of WIN Semi's
profitability.

WIN Semi: Valuation Methodology
Our price target (base-case value) increases from NT$600 to NT$700: This also factors
in our EPS forecast changes  for 2025 to 2028.
We continue to derive our price target from a residual income model as we believe it best
presents the value of the company. All of our other key assumptions are unchanged,
including a cost of equity of 8.0% (beta of 1.0, equity risk premium of 6% and risk-free

rate of 2%), intermediate growth rate assumption of 15% and terminal growth rate of
4.0%.
Our bull-case value  increases  from NT$900 to NT$1050 and our bear-case value from NT
$300 to NT$350, implying 44x and 14x 2028e EPS, respectively.

▪ Relatively strong iPhone demand (vs.
Android) appears likely in the near term, but
smartphone growth is mature compared to

AI demand.
▪ We expect to see more GaAs wafer
consumption as we migrate to the WiFi 7
era, as WiFi 7 has three bands (2.4 GHz, 5
GHz and 6 GHz) vs. WiFi 6 at just two (2.4
GHz, 5 GHz).
▪ GaN penetration in satellite business could
lift the wafer ASP.
▪ We think optical transceivers and robotics
may become future drivers, as WIN Semi has
the technology to enable products like EML,
CW-DFB and DML.
▪ We are OW given the strong margin
improvement from the new business.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
아직 수요의 끝은 보이지 않는다 
AI 투자에 대한 우려의 목소리도 있지만 정작 부품을 공급하고 투자하는 쪽의 목소
리는 시간이 갈수록 더 커지고 있다. 공급 부족의 시점 매 분기 뒤로 밀려나고 있기 
때문이다. 28년까지 공급부족이라고 했던 메모리업체들은 이구동성으로 30년까지 공
급부족이 지속된다고 언급하고 있다. 이러한 변화는 실적에 고스란히 반영되고 있
다. 26년 3분기 메모리 영업이익만 108.5조원으로 추정한다. 4 분기에는 140조원에 
이를 것으로 전망한다. 
HBM4와 QLC는 차별화 포인트 
삼성전자는 HBM에서 입지가 좋았던 적이 없었다. 점유율, 수익성, 가동률, 제품 성
능, 고객 등 모든 항목에서 경쟁사 대비 열위에 있었다. HBM4는 모든 항목에서 입
지를 개선시킬 변수가 될 것으로 예상한다. 26년 3분기 물량은 경쟁사 수준일 것으
로 보이고 연간 물량도 유사한 수준일 전망이다. 27년에는 가격 인상과 가동률 상승
으로 수익성도 크게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. QLC는 TLC와 함께 AI 모델 진화의 
수혜를 받을 제품으로 빠르게 부상하고 있다. 메타의 Muse 영향이다. 접속자당 가
상 메모리를 NAND에 100GB를 할당하고 있기 때문이다.  
부족한 주주환원으로 투자 우선 순위에서 밀릴 듯 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 
변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, HBM 
시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 
판단하기 때문이다. 목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황
을 고려하면 적정 주가는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치
에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 
제한적일 것으로 판단하기 때문이다.

26년 3분기 매출액은 195.3조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.9% 증가한 195.3조원이다. VD/
가전을 제외한 주요 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 증가했다. DS, MX 모두 물량보다는 
가격 효과가 더 크게 작용한 것으로 분석된다. 사업부별로는  
1) DS 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 15.4% 증가한 147.1조원이다. 메모리
는 26년 2분기 대비 16.4% 증가했다. 26년 3분기 DRAM B/G(Bit Growth)
는 +2.0%, ASP는 +20.0%이다. NAND B/G는 +5.0%, ASP는 +18.0%이다. 
HBM 물량 증가는 HBM4가 주도했고, 그 이하 제품 비중은 하락한 것으로 
추정한다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 7.1% 증가한 8조원이다. 소형
은 해외 고객 및 국내 고객의 신제품 출시 영향으로 ASP 상승, 물량 증가 
효과가 동반되었고, 대형은 게이밍 모니터 비중을 확대했다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.4% 증가한 37.7조원이
다. MX는 15.5% 증가했다. 물량은 소폭 감소했고, ASP 상승 영향을 받았다. 
네트워크 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 60% 감소했다.  
4) VD/가전 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 4.1% 감소한 13.9조원이다. VD 
매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 6.5% 감소했다. 가전은 26년 2분기 대비 1.3% 
감소했다.

26년 3분기 영업이익은 107.4조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 20% 증가한 107.4조원이다. DS,  
디스플레이와 MX는 2분기 대비 증가했고, VD/가전은 2분기 대비 감소했다. 사업부별
로는 
1) DS 사업부는 26년 2분기 대비 19.4% 증가한 106.5조원이다. 2분기 대비 영
업이익률은 소폭 상승했다. 메모리 영업이익은 2분기 대비 19.0% 증가했고, 
비메모리는 영업적자를 유지했다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 54.5% 증가한 1.1조원이다. 
중소형 신제품 효과로 수익성이 개선된 영향이다. 대형도 믹스 개선으로 수
익성이 개선되었다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자폭이 감소했다. 물량
은 줄었지만 High End 비중 상승으로 ASP는 개선되었고, 원가 비중이 낮아
진 영향이다. 
4) VD/가전 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자 규모가 커졌다.

삼성전자 (005930)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기 매출액은 226.5조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 16.0% 증가한 226.5조원으로 예상
한다. DS가 증가하고 나머지는 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS는 메모리가 성장을 주도할 것으로 기대한다. 가격과 물량 모두 3분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 전망한다. Context Server에 대한 수요가 증가하고, Personal Agent의 가상메
모리 설정으로 인해 NAND 수요가 증가하면서 QLC에 대한 수요 증가 속도가 빨라질 
전망이다. 이로 인해 NAND Bit Growth는 경쟁사와 차별화된 수준일 것으로 예상한
다.  
HBM 물량도 꾸준히 증가할 것으로 기대한다. 3 분기에도 HBM4를 중심으로 물량이 
크게 증가했다. HBM4는 SK하이닉스와 비슷한 물량일 것으로 예상한다. 아직 전체 
물량은 SK하이닉스가 더 많은 상황이고, HBM 내 HBM4 비중은 업계 최고 수준인 것
으로 판단한다. 26년까지는 HBM이 수익성에는 마이너스 요인이지만, 27년에는 
DRAM 수준의 영업이익이 가능할 것으로 기대한다.  
MX는 비수기 저가 라인 물량 조정으로 전체 물량은 감소하지만, 제품믹스는 개선되
어 판가는 3분기 대비 상승할 것으로 예상한다.

26년 4분기 영업이익은 138.2조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 28.7% 증가한 138.2조원으로 예
상한다. DS 사업부를 제외하고 모두 3분기 대비 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS 사업부 영업이익은 3분기 대비 30% 증가한 138.5조원으로 전망한다. DRAM, 
NAND 가격이 10% 초중반 수준으로 상승하고, 물량은 10% 중반 이상 증가하면서 3
분기 대비 영업이익률 개선을 기대하기 때문이다. HBM 물량 증가가 이익 개선에 미
치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상한다.  
디스플레이, MX는 물량 부진으로 3분기 대비 영업이익은 부진할 것으로 전망한다.

DS가 성장 주도 
DS만 보자

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
Check Point 
1. 모자란 듯한 주주환원 
26년 3분기 실적 발표에서 밝힌 주주환원 정책은 지난 8월의 내용과 다르지 않다. 3
분기 배당 30조원(주당 4,601원)을 확정한 정도이다. 잔여 배당 재원이 55~75조원으
로 구체화했고, 현금 배당과 자사주 매입을 병행하겠다고 밝혔다.  
자사주 매입 시 우선주 매입도 고려 대상이라고 언급한 것은 고무적이지만 27년부터 
새롭게 시작되는 주주환원 정책을 결정하기 위한 이사회 개최는 27년 1월에 열릴 것
으로 예고하였기에 연내 추가적인 주주환원 발표는 없을 것으로 판단한다.  
2. HBM4로 HBM 시장에서 입지 강화 
HBM4를 통해서 자신감을 회복하고 있는 것으로 판단한다. 양산 시점은 경쟁사 대비 
빨랐고, 3분기 물량은 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준인 것으로 추정한다. HBM3e가 부진했기 
때문에 전체 생산량에서 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 가장 높은 수준일 것으로 추정한다.  
26년 전체 물량도 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준이 될 것으로 전망한다. 삼성전자는 HBM 시
장 점유율을 DRAM 수준으로 맞추겠다는 목표를 3분기에 달성한 것으로 밝혔다.  
물량이 증가하면서 가동률도 빠르게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. 아직은 DRAM과 영업
이익률에서 큰 차이가 있지만 27년에는 인상된 가격, 높아진 수율, 가동률 개선으로 
DRAM과 영업이익률 차이를 좁힐 수 있을 것으로 기대한다.  
3. QLC NAND 시장 확대 
AI 컴퓨팅 확산은 NAND에게도 성장 기회이다. AI 모델들의 Context Window가 길어
져 KV Cache 메모리에 대한 수요가 커지면서 High Endurance TLC 수요가 급증하고 
있다. DRAM을 대체할 수준의 NAND가 필요하게 되었기 때문이다.  
이 뿐만 아니라 고용량 NAND에 대한 수요도 Personal Agent의 출현으로 급속히 증
가할 것으로 기대한다. 여기에는 QLC NAND가 주로 사용된다. 메타의 Muse는 접속
자 1인에 대해서 DRAM 8GB, NAND 100GB의 가상 메모리를 설정하고 운영한다.  
Agent AI의 확산은 스토리지에 대한 수요도 급격히 확대할 것으로 기대하고, 삼성전
자에게도 기회가 될 것으로 기대한다.  
26년 4분기 NAND Bit Growth는 삼성전자가 가장 높을 것으로 예상한다.

자사주 관련 일부

### Page 8

삼성전자 (005930)

8 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 350,000원으로 하향 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 
1) 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 
2) 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, 
3) HBM 시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 
4) 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 판단하기 때문이다. 
목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황을 고려하면 적정 주가
는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사
와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 제한적일 것으로 판단하기 
때문이다.

표 5. 삼성전자 이론적 적정 주가는 465,690원 
투자 지표 내용 비고 
Rf(%) 5.0

베타 1.25 52주(주간) 
g(%) 5.0

ROE(%) 53.4  2026년 ROE 
적정 PBR(배) 4.3  PBR=(ROE-g)/(Ke-g)

Document 267: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적LG에너지솔루션(373220) 보상금 제외해도 호실적보상금 제외해도 호실적 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

(373220) 
LG 에너지솔루션 
2026.10.12

에너지/인프라/배터리 황성현, Ph.D. 
6878 / tjdgus2009@eugenefn.com

보상금 제외해도 호실적 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 530,000원(상향) 
 현재주가 401,000원(10/8)

 [투자의견] 당사는 LG에너지솔루션에 대해 실적 추정치 상향을 반영하여 목
표주가를 530,000원으로 13% 상향하고, 투자의견 BUY를 유지한다. 
3Q26부터 ESS 물량 증가에 따른 가동률 상승 효과가 본격적으로 실적에 
반영되기 시작했으며, 4Q26에도 AMPC 제외 흑자 기조를 이어갈 전망이
다. 전사 가동률은 3분기 65%까지 상승한 것으로 파악되며, 이에 따른 펀
더멘털 개선이 본격화되고 있다고 판단한다. 
 [3Q26 잠정 실적 Review] 매출액 9.6조원(+28%qoq, +59%yoy), 영업이
익 7,560억원(+567%qoq, +26%yoy)을 기록하며 당사 추정을 큰 폭으로 
상회했다. 북미 생산보조금(AMPC)을 제외한 매출액은 9.2조원, 영업이익은 
3,391억원(영업이익률 3.7%)으로, 2 분기(-1,277억원, -1.7%) 대비 흑자 
전환에 성공했다. ① 원통형은 테슬라향 물량 호조에 힘입어 영업이익 
2,010억원(영업이익률 8.1%)을 달성한 것으로 추정된다. ② EV는 유럽 폴
란드 공장의 가동률 상승과 GM 얼티엄의 8월 가동 개시로 매출이 증가했
다. 여기에 완성차 업체 보상금 3,000억원이 반영되며 영업이익 1,090억
원(영업이익률 2.5%)을 기록한 것으로 추정된다. ③ ESS는 매출액이 50% 
이상 급증하며 실적 서프라이즈를 견인했다. AMPC 포함 영업이익은 4,460
억원(영업이익률 16.0%)으로 추정된다. 2 분기 매출 성장률 +17%qoq에 
이어 3분기 성장세가 더욱 가팔라졌다는 점이 긍정적이다. 
 [4Q26 Preview] 매출액 8.0조원(-17%qoq, +30%yoy), 영업이익 6,790
억원(-10%qoq, 흑전yoy)으로 추정한다. 4 분기 실적은 ESS 수익성 개선 
속도와 완성차 업체 보상금 반영 규모에 따라 변동할 전망이다. 동사는 기존 
가이던스를 통해 하반기 북미 ESS 생산량이 상반기 대비 2배 증가할 것으
로 제시한 바 있다. 3분기 ESS는 AMPC를 제외하더라도 양호한 수익성을 
기록한 것으로 추정된다. 향후 팩 생산 병목 해소와 북미 공장 가동 안정화
에 따른 고정비 부담 완화가 이어지며 추가적인 손익 개선이 가능할 전망이
다. EV 역시 GM 얼티엄 생산 확대와 유럽 공장 가동률 상승이 지속되며 
AMPC 제외 적자 규모를 점진적으로 축소할 것으로 예상한다. 
 [투자포인트] 9월 미시간 랜싱 공장의 개소식이 개최됐다. 랜싱 공장은 당초 
EV용 파우치 배터리 생산거점으로 계획됐으나 ESS 중심으로 역할이 재편
됐으며, 내년에는 10GWh 이상의 각형 LFP 생산라인이 추가될 예정이다. 
2027년에는 신규 대규모 증설보다 기존 북미 생산거점의 가동률 상승과 
ESS 생산량 확대를 통한 수익성 개선이 핵심이 될 전망이다. 북미 전력망 
접속 지연과 AI 데이터센터의 부하 변동성 확대에 따라 Stand-alone ESS, 
BTM, UPS, BBU 수요가 증가하면서 신규 수주도 지속 확대될 것으로 예상
한다. 특히 동사의 Non-PFE 요건 충족과 SI· O&M을 아우르는 End-to-
End 솔루션 역량이 북미 ESS 시장에서 경쟁력으로 작용할 전망이다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에 동 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

Document 287: AMD电话会：Q4 AI芯片收入将首次超越服务器CPU，明年数据中心收入占比将破80%

Source PDF: all report pdf/AMD电话会：Q4 AI芯片收入将首次超越服务器CPU，明年数据中心收入占比将破80%.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

AMD CEO 苏姿丰指出 AI 代理正成为云端 CPU 的 “ 下⼀个杀⼿级应⽤ ” ，且 Q4 数据中⼼ AI 芯
⽚收⼊将历史性地⾸次超越传统服务器 CPU 。尽管预计明年 PC 市场将出现两位数下滑，但
凭借 AI 算⼒基础设施的爆发式需求， AMD 正迎来盈利能⼒和利润率的全⾯扩张。
摘要：
数据中⼼业务爆发式增⻓推动 AMD 业绩全⾯超预期，公司上调明年展望，预计服务器收⼊翻
番、 AI 芯⽚收⼊增速更快。
华尔街⻅闻提及，美东时间 3 ⽇周⼆美股盘后， AMD 公布， 2026 年第三季度营收同⽐增⻓ 45%
⾄ 133.77 亿美元，⼜创单季新⾼，较分析师预期⾼约 2.3% 。⾮ GAAP ⼝径下，每股收益同⽐增
⻓ 74% ⾄ 2.09 美元，较分析师预期⾼逾 6% 。
随后电话会上， AMD ⾸席执⾏官苏姿丰（ Lisa Su ）表⽰，数据中⼼需求正在加速，预计 2027 年
数据中⼼营收将超过此前预期，服务器营收届时将实现翻倍，占总营收⽐例最⾼可达 80%。
她表⽰，公司 " 对未来数年保持卓越营收增⻓的能⼒抱有强烈信⼼，且盈利增速将超过营收增

速 "。苏姿丰在回应分析师提问时坦⾔，当前需求确实⾼于供给，公司正与晶圆、基板、先进封
装、存储及其他关键元器件供应商深度合作以提升产能，并表⽰公司 " 有信⼼ " 凭借与⽣态系统的
深厚合作关系进⼀步扩⼤供给。
在产品战略层⾯， AMD 正同步推进 CPU 与 AI 加速芯⽚两条产品线的更新换代。苏姿丰透露，
EPYC Venice 处理器已于三季度开始出货，⾸批 OEM 系统预计四季度正式上市。
在 AI 加速器⽅⾯， AMD 已启动 MI455X 的批量出货， Anthropic 、 Meta 、 Microsoft 、 OpenAI 和
Oracle 等头部客⼾在三季度完成了⾸批 Helios 机架的部署。苏姿丰称，客⼾正在 Helios 上运⾏最
新模型，整体性能表现令⼈满意，四季度 Helios 的部署规模将⼤幅增加。
此外苏姿丰明确警⽰，预计 2027 年 PC 市场将出现两位数百分⽐的降幅。
彭博援引 Emarketer 分析师 Jacob Bourne 的观点称，此次业绩结果 " 将给投资者带来⿎励，因为

投资者近期正越来越严格地审视 AI ⽀出及其回报 " ，尤其是在科技股和半导体板块经历持续调整
的背景下。
Q3 业绩创纪录， 2027 年总营收预期激增超 80%
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

AMD 在今年第三季度交出了⼀份堪称完美的答卷。当季总营收达到 134 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 45% ；
⾮公认会计准则下（ Non-GAAP ）每股收益（ EPS ）增幅更为惊⼈，同⽐⼤增 74% ⾄ 2.09 美
元。⽑利率也扩⼤⾄ 56% ，同⽐提升了 190 个基点。
真正引爆市场情绪的是管理层给出的远期指引。 AMD 预计第四季度营收将达到约 170 亿美元（同
⽐增⻓约 66% ）。更重要的是，公司对 2027 年给出了极其乐观的预期。
苏姿丰在会上表⽰：
她明确指出，2027 年数据中⼼业务将占到公司总营收的 80% 以上，成为驱动收⼊和盈利增⻓的
绝对主⼒。此外，服务器收⼊预计在 2027 年将实现同⽐翻倍。
在谈及市场最关⼼的供需问题时，苏姿丰直⾔：
历史性交叉： Q4 AI 芯⽚收⼊将超越服务器 CPU
数据中⼼业务是 AMD 本季度的核⼼引擎， Q3 收⼊同⽐暴涨 97% ⾄创纪录的 85 亿美元，占总营收
的⽐例从⼀年前的 47% 跃升⾄ 64% 。
这⼀增⻓背后，是新⼀代产品的全⾯铺开。 MI455X 在三季度开始批量出货，包括 Anthropic 、

Meta 、微软、 OpenAI 和甲⻣⽂在内的领先客⼾已经迅速上线了⾸批 Helios 机架。
对于接下来的第四季度， CFO 胡锦透露了⼀个关键的⾥程碑数据：
为了进⼀步加码 AI 军备竞赛， AMD 在三季度宣布了两项关键收购（ Taalas 和 World Labs ）。其
中， World Labs 的联合创始⼈兼 CEO 李⻜⻜将作为⾸席科学家加⼊ AMD ，领导模型研究并帮助
塑造未来的软硬件系统路线图。
“AI 代理正成为云端 CPU 的下⼀个杀⼿级应⽤ ”
在深度⻓篇的电话会中，苏姿丰多次提及⼀个核⼼词汇 ——Agentic Workloads （ AI 代理⼯作负
载），并将其视为重塑计算基础设施的核⼼⼒量。
苏姿丰在分享⾏业洞察时指出：
基于⽬前的可⻅度，我们现在预计 2027 年的营收将增⻓超过 80%，这是我们去年 11 ⽉在财务分
析师⽇上预期的⻓期财务模型的两倍多。
需求确实⾼于供应，因此我们继续与供应链合作伙伴合作以增加供应。鉴于我们在整个⽣态系统
中拥有的强⼤关系，我相信我们有很好的机会做到这⼀点。

第四季度数据中⼼业务环⽐将实现约 50% 的激增。实际上，这将是数据中⼼ AI 业务（ GPU 等）
收⼊超越服务器业务（ CPU ）的第⼀个季度。相较于服务器端，数据中⼼ AI 业务在 Q4 将出现⼀
个⾮常显著的阶跃式上升。

### Page 3

凭借在性能和总拥有成本（ TCO ）上的优势， EPYC 处理器正在疯狂抢占市场份额。苏姿丰特别
提及：
此外， AMD 最近推出的 Helion 获得了前所未有的早期需求， OEM 和 ODM 合作伙伴承诺提供的
Venice 平台数量⽐上⼀代多出 20% 。
隐忧与对策： PC 市场明年⾯临两位数衰退
在 AI 业务⼀路⾼歌猛进的同时， AMD 的客⼾端和游戏业务则⾯临着宏观环境的逆⻛。
第三季度，客⼾端收⼊同⽐增⻓ 7% ⾄ 2.9 亿美元，但环⽐下降了 4% 。苏姿丰对 PC 市场给出了直
⽩的预警：
⾯对 PC 市场的寒冬， AMD 选择避开低端市场的价格战。苏姿丰表⽰：
不过， PC 市场的疲软并未影响公司的整体盈利扩张逻辑。 CFO 胡锦强调，随着营收在 2027 年激
增超 80% ，公司的运营⽀出增速将不到营收增速的⼀半，这将带来 “ ⾮常显著的营业利润率扩张
和盈利能⼒提升 ” 。
AMD Q3 业绩电话全⽂（ AI 辅助翻译）：
云提供商在本季度增加了⼤量 EPYC （霄⻰）容量，⽤于个⼈和企业代理，这些代理正迅速成为

云端 CPU 的下⼀个杀⼿级应⽤。这些代理在应⽤程序和企业系统中连续并⾏地⼯作，在增加加
速器需求的同时，创造了对 CPU 需求的急剧增加。采⽤才刚刚开始，我们预计随着代理被更⼴
泛地部署并承担更复杂的⼯作，需求将急剧增⻓。
OpenAI 选择了 Turin EPYC 处理器作为其⾸个定制加速器平台的主机 CPU 。
本季度末 PC 终端售出放缓，因为较⾼的内存和组件成本给需求带来了压⼒。我们在 2027 年的规
划假设是， PC 市场的单位出货量将出现两位数百分⽐的下降。
我们将专注于能够增加最⼤价值的细分市场，如企业级、⾼端消费级和台式机渠道。我们认识到
低端市场存在⼤量波动，坦率地说，这也是我们敞⼝最⼩的部分。
主持⼈：  欢迎参加 AMD 2026 年第三季度业绩电话会议。⽬前所有参会者处于仅收听模式，正
式演⽰结束后将进⾏问答环节。请注意，本次会议正在录⾳。

感谢 Matt ，也向今天收听的各位致以问候。第三季度是我们有史以来规模最⼤、盈利最强的季
度，这得益于我们开始推进下⼀代数据中⼼产品的量产。营收同⽐增⻓ 45% ，达到 134 亿美元，
创下纪录，主要驱动因素来⾃ EPYC 和 Instinct 的创纪录销售以及嵌⼊式业务的强劲增⻓。每股收
益增速更快，增⻓ 74% ，达到 2.09 美元。
本季度我们执⾏表现出⾊： Helios 量产按计划推进， EPYC 在云端、企业及超级智能⼯作负载中
的采⽤持续扩⼤。数据中⼼业务收⼊的环⽐增速在今年每个季度均持续加快，该业务板块在第三
季度贡献了总营收的 64% ，⽽⼀年前这⼀⽐例仅为 47% 。
我们预计这⼀加速态势将在第四季度延续，主要驱动⼒来⾃推理及智能体⼯作负载需求的增加，
以及最新产品的加速量产。
数据中⼼业务
数据中⼼业务收⼊同⽐增⻓ 97% ，达到创纪录的 85 亿美元。在服务器领域，云端和企业销售均
实现同⽐翻番以上。我们⼤幅超越了此前 " 增⻓逾 80%" 的业绩预期，实现了显著的收⼊份额提

升。
客⼾将 EPYC 部署到更多应⽤场景，智能体⼯作负载贡献了显著增⻓。在云端，超⼤规模云⼚商
在本季度部署了⼤量 EPYC 服务器，⽤于运⾏更多内部服务并扩展其公有云产品。 Turin ⽬前是公
有云中部署最⼴泛的 EPYC 代际产品，来⾃各⼤主要⼚商的 500 余个实例类型已⾯向全球开放。
云⼚商在本季度还新增了⼤量 EPYC 容量，⽤于⽀持个⼈和企业智能体 —— 这正迅速成为云端
CPU 的下⼀个 " 杀⼿级应⽤ " 。
Meta 的 Muse 和 OpenAI 的 Dots 展⽰了个⼈智能体的快速增⻓势头 ——Muse 智能体在上线前六周
内激活⽤⼾已超 1000 万。企业客⼾同样⾏动迅速，正在软件开发、供应链和 IT 运营等领域积极
部署智能体。这些智能体持续并⾏运⾏于各类应⽤和企业系统之中，在推动加速芯⽚需求增⻓的
同时，也显著带动了 CPU 需求的提升。⽬前相关部署仍处于起步阶段，随着智能体的更⼴泛部
署和承担更复杂任务，我们预期需求将⼤幅增⻓。

### Page 5

在头节点领域，我们在本季度赢得了多家顶尖模型开发商和云服务商的重要部署合同，这些合同
将在未来⼏个季度陆续落地。 EPYC 领先的性能表现和成熟的⽣态系统，已经在⼤规模⽣产环境
中得到充分验证，使其成为定制加速平台⾸选的主机 CPU 。以 OpenAI 为例，其⾸批⾃研加速器
平台即选⽤了 Turin EPYC 处理器作为主机 CPU 。
在企业市场， EPYC 服务器渠道销量创下历史纪录，来⾃新⽼客⼾的需求均⼗分强劲。本季度，
我们新增了数⼗家⾸次采⽤ EPYC 的客⼾，涵盖⼤型⾦融服务、通信、零售和医疗健康企业。现
有客⼾同样在将 EPYC 确⽴为其主要计算平台，从特定⼯作负载的部署拓展⾄更⼤⽐例的整体基
础设施覆盖。
在第三季度，我们已开始向客⼾发货 EPYC Venice 处理器，⾸批 OEM 系统也将于本季度正式上
市。从初期需求来看， Venice 是迄今为⽌我们在新⼀代 EPYC 上看到的最强劲反响。 OEM 和
ODM 合作伙伴已确认超过 240 个 Venice 平台，⽐ Turin 多出 20% ；云⼚商也已于本季度开始部署

Venice ，相关实例计划于 2027 年初正式推出。
我们近期发布了 Helion—— ⼀套基于 Venice 的参考设计，可为智能体基础设施提供交钥匙式
EPYC 机架级系统。客⼾可针对各⾃⼯作负载对 Helion 进⾏定制：从为超⼤规模云⼚商提供全
CPU 机架，到企业级的 Venice 与 Instinct GPU 混合系统 —— 后者可同时运⾏智能体和模型。在全
CPU 配置下，单台 Helion 机架可搭载多达 144 颗 Venice 处理器，拥有近 74,000 个线程，实现业界
最⾼的智能体计算密度。各⼤主要服务器 OEM 和 ODM 均计划提供 Helion 产品，⾸批系统将于本
季度出货。
放眼更宏观的市场，服务器计算需求正在云端、企业、智能体及超级智能基础设施领域全⾯加
速。 EPYC 覆盖所有这些市场，拥有业界最宽泛的产品组合 —— 针对云端和智能体的单路性能领
先，针对企业和头节点的单核性能领先，以及针对不同⼯作负载优化的内存与 IO 配置选项。
EPYC 在此基础上进⼀步以显著更优的性能功耗⽐、性能核⼼⽐和性能价格⽐领跑所有其他 x86

及 ARM 服务器 CPU 。随着 TAM （总可寻址市场）预计到 2030 年增⻓⾄约 2200 亿美元，这将使我
们有能⼒持续扩⼤市场份额并显著壮⼤服务器业务。
数据中⼼ AI 业务
前沿实验室、超⼤规模云⼚商和云服务商扩⼤了 Instinct 在推理和训练领域的部署，推动营收创
历史新⾼。
本季度我们开始⼤批量出货 MI455X ，包括 Anthropic 、 Meta 、微软、 OpenAI 和 Oracle 在内的核
⼼客⼾已迅速完成了⾸批 Helios 机架的上线部署。客⼾⽬前正在 Helios 上运⾏其最新模型和⼯作
负载，初期部署表现出⾊。
随着产能持续提升，以及 HPE 、戴尔、联想、超微等 OEM 伙伴陆续开始出货系统，我们预计
Helios 的部署量将在第四季度⼤幅增加。 MI455X 在计算算⼒、内存容量和横向扩展带宽⽅⾯均
处于⾏业领先地位，⽽ Helios 上市后，其在内存带宽和纵向扩展带宽⽅⾯相较竞争对⼿的优势将
进⼀步拉⼤。
我们正与制造和 OEM 合作伙伴密切协作，随客⼾扩⼤部署规模同步扩充产能。

在超级计算领域， EuroHPC 选择 Instinct MI430X GPU 和第六代 EPYC 处理器为 LUMI-AI 提供算
⼒⽀持 ——LUMI-AI 是欧洲计划于 2027 年下半年上线的下⼀代旗舰超算。 MI430X 的双精度计算
性能接近竞品的九倍，这对科学模拟⾄关重要。 MI430X ⽬前已被选⽤于全球多台新建超算系
统，随着各国政府和科研机构规划新⼀轮重⼤投资，我们也处于有利位置赢取更多订单。
在软件⽅⾯，我们发布了 ROCm 10 ，针对⼤规模⽣产部署带来重⼤升级，包括⼤型 GPU 集群的
加速启动以及针对⻓时间运⾏⼯作负载的更⾼韧性。 ROCm 10 还新增了 ROCm.ai 智能体性能调
优功能，可对⼯作负载进⾏分析，识别瓶颈，并⾃动应⽤和验证优化⽅案，将以往需要数周的⼯

展望与前瞻
放眼未来，我们的核⼼任务是⼤规模落地执⾏。 Venice 、 MI450 系列和 Helios 的量产均处于早期
阶段，三条产品线均有望继续⼤幅增⻓。
⽬前，我们预计数据中⼼业务收⼊增速将在第四季度进⼀步加快，全年业务收⼊将较 2025 年实
现翻番以上。我们对 2027 年的前景判断也在持续增强。
上季度，我们曾表⽰预计年度服务器收⼊增⻓超过 70% ，数据中⼼业务全年收⼊实现翻番。此
后，客⼾扩⼤了 EPYC 和 Instinct 的部署计划，我们也进⼀步落实了更多供货资源以⽀撑增⻓。⽬
前，我们预计 2027 年服务器收⼊将实现同⽐翻番，数据中⼼ AI 收⼊的增速也将超过 90 天前的预
期。因此，我们预计数据中⼼业务在 2027 年将占到总营收的 80% 以上，并成为营收和盈利增⻓
的主要驱动⼒。
放眼更⻓远， MI500 的客⼾接洽持续进展顺利，市场兴趣极为浓厚，因为这⼜是⼀次性能上的重
⼤跨越。我们正与多家前沿实验室开展深度合作，针对其⼯作负载对 MI500 进⾏优化，这些客⼾

正在规划多吉⽡规模的部署⽅案。
客⼾与游戏业务
客⼾与游戏业务收⼊同⽐下降 8% ，⾄ 37 亿美元。其中客⼾业务收⼊同⽐增⻓ 7% ，⾄ 29 亿美
元，主要受 Ryzen 移动处理器强劲销售拉动。商⽤ PC 正成为我们客⼾业务中越来越重要的组成
部分，⼤型企业正在全体员⼯中推⼴部署 Ryzen Pro 商⽤ PC 。全球⼗⼤银⾏中有 8 家、全球⼗⼤
医疗健康和汽⻋企业中有 7 家，⽬前均已采⽤ Ryzen Pro 商⽤ PC 。 OEM ⼚商持续扩展基于 AMD
芯⽚的商⽤产品线，从⽇常商务 PC 到⾼端⼯作站，惠普、联想等⼚商已推出搭载最新 Ryzen AI
Max Pro 400 系列处理器的商⽤笔记本电脑和⼩尺⼨台式机，这些系统可实现⼯作站级性能，并
能在本地运⾏参数量⾼达 3000 亿的超级智能模型。
从市场整体来看，受内存和组件成本上升拖累需求， PC 销售在季末有所放缓。我们正在相应调
整业务节奏，使进货量与销售量保持⼀致，以保持客⼾和渠道库存的健康⽔平。在 2027 年的规

划中，我们预计 PC 市场出货量将出现两位数百分⽐的下滑。在此环境下，我们将凭借持续迭代
的产品路线图和不断扩⼤的商⽤市场份额来跑赢⼤盘。
游戏业务收⼊同⽐下降 40% ，⾄ 7.73 亿美元，主要受控制台周期当前阶段半定制芯⽚销售下滑影
响，符合预期。游戏显卡收⼊同⽐下滑，但 Radeon GPU 渠道销量环⽐有所增⻓。在游戏业务以
外， Radeon AI Pro 收⼊较上季度⼤幅增⻓，越来越多的开发者选择 AMD 在本地运⾏和微调模
型。受单位性能价格⽐领先优势及针对最新开源模型优化的开放软件栈驱动，我们预计 Radeon
AI Pro 将延续强劲增⻓势头。
嵌⼊式业务

### Page 7

嵌⼊式业务收⼊同⽐增⻓ 29% ，⾄ 11 亿美元，实现连续第四个季度的加速增⻓。各终端市场需
求⼗分强劲，以航空航天与国防、汽⻋、通信和⼯业客⼾的增⻓最为突出。数据中⼼建设的持续
推进也在控制平⾯和光学⽹络应⽤中拉动了对嵌⼊式 CPU 和⾃适应 SoC 的更多需求。
我们继续推进 2026 年超过 180 亿美元设计订单的落地⽬标，这将是我们连续第三个创纪录年。⾃
收购赛灵思以来，⾃适应设计订单每年均实现增⻓，嵌⼊式 x86 和半定制订单的增速更快。随着
这些订单在未来⼏年陆续量产，我们预计嵌⼊式业务将实现强劲的两位数年增⻓，并持续跑赢市
场。
总结与展望
综上所述，我们交出了⼀份优异的第三季度成绩单，并预计随着数据中⼼和嵌⼊式业务增⻓对低
迷客⼾ CPU 销售的充分对冲，第四季度将更为强劲。
我们以有史以来最强的产品组合和显著的客⼾动能进⼊ 2027 年。我们已联动整个供应链，锁定
了⽀撑明年更⾼增⻓所需的晶圆、基板、先进封装、内存及其他关键组件资源。
基于当前的业务可⻅度，我们预计 2027 年营收增⻓将超过 80% ，这是我们去年 11 ⽉分析师⽇上

所披露的⻓期财务模型⽬标值的两倍以上。数据中⼼业务将是最⼤贡献者，驱动⼒来⾃ Helios 部
署的持续扩⼤以及 EPYC 市场份额的持续提升。我们同样预计嵌⼊式业务明年将加速增⻓，受益
于⾃适应架构及 x86 解决⽅案的⼴泛采⽤。
展望 2027 年以后，我们正在加⼤硅、软件和系统路线图的研发投⼊，并与最⼤客⼾紧密协作，
共同⽀撑他们未来三⾄五年的宏观基础设施规划。我们也在深化与供应链合作伙伴的战略协作，
并与他们共同投资，以锁定⽀撑我们⻓期增⻓所需的产能。
综合来看，这⼀切让我们对未来数年持续实现卓越营收增⻓、盈利增速更快的⽬标充满信⼼。

第三季度财务业绩回顾
第三季度业绩表现出⾊，营收达到创纪录的 134 亿美元，超出指引区间上限，主要受数据中⼼业

务板块驱动 —— 该板块收⼊同⽐近乎翻番。
营收环⽐增⻓ 16% ，主要受数据中⼼和嵌⼊式业务板块强劲增⻓拉动，但被客⼾和游戏业务板块
的下滑部分抵消。数据中⼼业务板块约占总营收的 64% ，较上季度的 58% 进⼀步提升，彰显了我
们业务结构的持续优化及其对 AMD 增⻓贡献的不断增强。
⽑利率为 56% ，同⽐提升 190 个基点，反映出更优化的产品结构。运营⽀出为 36 亿美元，同⽐增
⻓ 29% ，主要因我们持续加⼤研发投⼊，以扩展数据中⼼硅、系统及软件能⼒。运营利润同⽐增
⻓ 75% ，达到创纪录的 39 亿美元，增速⼤幅超越营收增速。运营利润率为 29% ，同⽐提升逾 5 个
百分点，彰显了业务规模扩⼤带来的运营杠杆效应。
第三季度，我们开始按会计规则确认此前公告的客⼾协议项下所发⾏认股权证的相关会计影响
—— 该影响于我们开始出货 MI450 系列 GPU 时产⽣。我们的 GAAP 摊薄每股收益为 1.46 美元，其
中包含约 11 亿美元的⾮现⾦⽀出（计⼊其他收⼊与⽀出项），主要反映公司股价上涨及认股权证

触发条件满⾜概率估算值变化的影响。

### Page 8

未来，随着我们和客⼾在该协议下持续推进，这⼀季度性⾮现⾦公允价值调整预计将有所增加，
每季度的具体⾦额将因 AMD 股价及其他估值假设的变化⽽有所不同。这些⾮现⾦调整不影响⾮
GAAP 业绩及⾃由现⾦流。
我们的⾮ GAAP 摊薄每股收益为 2.09 美元。
各业务板块情况
数据中⼼板块：  收⼊达到创纪录的 85 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 97% ，主要受第五代 EPYC 处理器强劲
需求拉动服务器收⼊创纪录，以及 AMD Instinct MI450 系列 GPU 发布带动数据中⼼ AI 收⼊创纪
录。环⽐来看，数据中⼼收⼊增⻓ 27% ，主要由 MI450 系列初期销售及强劲的服务器收⼊增⻓驱
动。数据中⼼板块运营利润为 29 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 168% ，运营利润率由⼀年前的 25% 提升⾄
34% ，彰显出数据中⼼业务的强⼤运营杠杆效应。
客⼾与游戏板块：  收⼊为 37 亿美元，同⽐下降 8% ，主因半定制及显卡销售下滑，部分被最新⼀
代 Ryzen 移动处理器需求增⻓所抵消。其中客⼾业务收⼊ 29 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 7% ，受 Ryzen 移

动处理器销量提升驱动；环⽐来看，受内存成本上升拖累 PC 出货量，客⼾业务收⼊下降 4% ，但
⾼端处理器产品结构改善形成了⼀定对冲。游戏业务收⼊ 7.73 亿美元，同⽐下降 40% ，主因半定
制收⼊下降及显卡需求疲软；环⽐来看，游戏收⼊基本持平。客⼾与游戏板块运营利润为 5.73 亿
美元，占板块收⼊的 15% ；去年同期为 8.67 亿美元，占⽐ 21% 。
嵌⼊式板块：  收⼊为 11 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 29% ，环⽐增⻓ 14% ，多个终端市场需求增强，航空
航天与国防、汽⻋、通信及⼯业终端市场增⻓最为突出。嵌⼊式板块运营利润 4.53 亿美元，占板
块收⼊的 41% ；去年同期为 2.83 亿美元，占⽐ 33% 。
资产负债表与现⾦流
本季度，库存环⽐增加约 30 亿美元⾄ 115 亿美元，以满⾜强劲的数据中⼼需求。经营活动产⽣的
现⾦流量为 12 亿美元，⾃由现⾦流为 5.54 亿美元，反映出营运资本投⼊增加，以及为⽀撑数据
中⼼业务快速增⻓⽽进⾏的持续资本⽀出，包括专⻔服务数据中⼼需求的后端产能投资。我们预

计将继续加⼤库存和产能投⼊，以⽀撑 2027 年的强劲增⻓。季末现⾦及现⾦等价物和短期投资
合计 158 亿美元，总债务 71 亿美元，其中包括今年 8 ⽉完成的 47.5 亿美元优先票据发⾏。
2026 年第四季度业绩展望
预计营收约为 170 亿美元，上下浮动 3% 。指引中值代表同⽐营收增速约为 66% ，主要由数据中
⼼业务板块的加速增⻓驱动。环⽐来看，预计营收将增⻓约 27% ，其中数据中⼼业务板块将实现
约 50% 的⼤幅环⽐增⻓，客⼾与游戏业务板块预计环⽐下降逾 20% ，嵌⼊式业务板块预计实现两
位数增⻓。
此外，第四季度⾮ GAAP ⽑利率预计约为 55% ，⾮ GAAP 运营⽀出预计约为 37.5 亿美元，⾮
GAAP 净利息及其他⽀出预计为约 400 万美元的净收益，⾮ GAAP 有效税率预计为 13% ，摊薄股
份数预计约为 16.7 亿股（已包含 Taalas 和 World Labs 收购预计于第四季度完成的估算影响），第
四季度股份数量预计不受客⼾认股权证的影响。

结语
第三季度是 AMD 的卓越季度，营收创历史纪录，运营利润率显著提升，盈利实现⼤幅增⻓，彰
显了公司整体的严谨执⾏⼒。随着数据中⼼业务规模持续扩⼤，我们的盈利能⼒和⻓期股东价值
将得到充分提升。

主持⼈：  感谢各位。现在进⼊问答环节，请在座各位提问。

们的出发点是：在投资增⻓的这⼀⾸要原则指导下，有策略地、有选择性地推进此类合作。

Jean Hu ，执⾏副总裁、⾸席财务官兼财务主管：  Josh ，感谢提问。在运营⽀出⽅⾯，正如你
所说，我们⼀直在积极投⼊。⽬前来看，这些投⼊正在转化为加速增⻓的收⼊。 2026 年运营杠
杆表现令我们相当满意 —— 收⼊增速⼤幅超越运营⽀出增速，推动运营利润率提升。从第四季度

增速的⼀半，这将推动 2027 年运营利润率实现⼤幅提升。

⽑利率⽅⾯，我们对整体情况相当满意。第三季度，我们正在推进 MI450 的产能爬坡，数据中⼼
AI 业务的表现实际上超出了我们进⼊季度时的预期。第四季度，数据中⼼业务将环⽐增⻓ 50% ，
⽽这正是数据中⼼ AI 业务超越服务器业务的季度 —— 众所周知， AI 业务对公司整体⽑利率有⼀定
的稀释效应，但⽑利润总额正在⼤幅增⻓。
展望 2027 年，数据中⼼业务将占到公司总收⼊的 80% 以上，业务结构的变化将对公司⽑利率产
⽣重⼤影响。数据中⼼ AI 业务将在全年持续放量，服务器业务预计实现翻番，不同季度之间的业
务结构差异会导致⽑利率出现⼀定波动。嵌⼊式业务在 2027 年的增强，也将有助于提升整体⽑
利率。

综合来看，我们的⽬标是实现强劲的营收增⻓和⽑利润增⻓，同时在运营⽀出增速低于营收增速
的驱动下，显著扩⼤运营利润率和整体盈利能⼒。

Jean Hu ，执⾏副总裁、⾸席财务官兼财务主管：  感谢提问。简单来说，认股权证包含三个部
分。
第⼀部分是净收⼊影响。我们报告的 Q3 实际营收、 Q4 指引及此后⼀切收⼊，均已是净收⼊，已
按授予⽇公允价值相应扣除了认股权证的收⼊减免部分，⼤家⽆需单独处理。

摊薄股份数 —— 即需要完成指定出货量并满⾜若⼲商业条件。待进⼊ 2027 年后，我们将就预计
达到归属和⾏权条件的时间节点提供明确指引，届时会有充分的透明度。
第三部分是季度性⾮现⾦公允价值重估⽀出，不影响⾮ GAAP 业绩及⾃由现⾦流。股份数量⽅
⾯，请等待我们进⼊ 2027 年后的明确指引。

### Page 12

Stacy Rasgon ：  你们好，感谢接受提问。您提到第四季度 GPU 将在 50% 的指引背景下超过
CPU ，请问这⼀超越的量级如何 —— 是微微超过，还是⼤幅领先？我想对明年的⼊⼝数量级有
个基本判断。
Jean Hu ，执⾏副总裁、⾸席财务官兼财务主管：  Stacy ，我来给⼤家⼀些参考。 MI450 的量产
爬坡是⼀个从 Q3 到 Q4 的⼤幅跃升，服务器业务也增⻓得⾮常强劲；但数据中⼼ AI 业务超过服务
器，是⼀次⼗分显著的台阶式提升。第四季度，数据中⼼ AI 的收⼊（以美元计）将远⾼于服务器
业务。
Stacy Rasgon：  是按美元计算的吗？
Jean Hu ，执⾏副总裁、⾸席财务官兼财务主管：  是的，按美元计算。
Stacy Rasgon：  好的。再问⼀个后续问题， Jean ：关于明年⽑利率，我不太确定如何解读您
之前的回答。能否给⼀个 2027 年⽑利率相对 Q4 ⽔平的⼤致⾛向指引 —— 包括年初和年末的合理
参考范围？
Jean Hu ，执⾏副总裁、⾸席财务官兼财务主管：  Stacy ，我们暂时⽆法提供逐季度的指引，主

要原因是 2027 年⽑利率的⾛势将由数据中⼼业务的内部结构所主导 —— 服务器将翻番， AI 业务
增速更快，两者的业务占⽐差异将在不同季度间形成⼀定的⽑利率波动。
⽬前来看， Q4 是⼀个较好的参考基准 —— 届时数据中⼼ AI 收⼊将远⾼于 CPU 收⼊。进⼊ 2027 年
后，不同季度的业务结构可能各有差异。我们同样受限于⽬前的供给可⻅度，正努⼒与供应商⼀
起争取更多供给。两个业务板块都将实现出⾊增⻓，这⼀点是确定的，但具体的季度结构分布⽬
前尚不清晰。
第七问： Joseph Moore （摩根⼠丹利）
Joseph Moore ：  感谢。我们听到了很多关于 Helion 机架的积极反馈。在全 CPU 配置下，这⼀
产品针对智能体⼯作负载与传统机架有何不同？以及您预计 GPU ⼑⽚的附加部署⽐例有多⾼？
Lisa Su ，董事⻓兼⾸席执⾏官：  Joe ， Helion 机架确实引发了相当热烈的积极反响。核⼼出发
点在于，这类智能体机架需要根据所在环境进⾏深度优化。在超⼤规模云场景下，全 CPU 配

置、⾼密度部署是主流， Venice 作为单路性能最优的处理器，在满配全 CPU 的情况下可实现极
⾼的智能体计算密度。在企业场景下，则会出现 CPU 与 GPU 混合部署的需求，让⽤⼾能够真正
根据⼯作负载灵活优化配置。 Helion 已向开放计算项⽬（ OCP ）贡献了全套开放规范，完全开
放的机架设计也赢得了市场的积极评价。来⾃ OEM 、 ODM 和最终客⼾的反馈均⾮常正⾯。
Joseph Moore：  好的，感谢。再问⼀个问题：服务器实现 100% 增⻓的情况下，贵公司是否认
为基本满⾜了市场需求，还是 2027 年全年都会持续存在供不应求的情况？
Lisa Su ，董事⻓兼⾸席执⾏官：  Joe ，实现这样的增⻓本⾝意味着我们已满⾜了⼤量需求。服
务器 TAM 未来三四年的年均增速⼤约在 50% 左右，增⻓来源于三个领域：传统云端和企业、智
能体 CPU ⼯作负载和头节点。我们相信，在给出的约 100% 增⻓预测中，每个细分市场都在实现
份额提升。
我确实认为市场上还有更多需求尚待满⾜，我们正积极与供应链合作伙伴共同解决这⼀问题，同

时也要确保套⽚、 DDR5 等配套组件准备就绪。需求来源⾼度多元化，⼏乎覆盖了每⼀个正在扩
⼤ AMD 部署规模的客⼾，这是令⼈振奋的积极信号。我们会持续努⼒增加供给，满⾜这些不断
落实的增量需求。
第⼋问： Harsh Kumar （ BMO 资本）

### Page 13

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
Harsh Kumar ：  Lisa ，我想问⼀个关于 Helios 的问题。贵公司是全球仅有的两家出货完整机架
系统的⼚商之⼀。请问您对市场份额的⽬标是什么？您在服务器市场与强劲竞争对⼿的份额争夺
上取得了出⾊成绩，请问在数据中⼼ GPU 及整机架领域有何雄⼼？另外，贵公司是否有意进⼊
企业市场？
Lisa Su ，董事⻓兼⾸席执⾏官：  Harsh ，我们对 Helios 的进展⾮常满意。整机架级解决⽅案是
重⼤的研发投⼊， Helios 的产能爬坡⾮常顺利：第三季度是良好的初始季度，第四季度和 2027
年持续放量。我们的⽬标是成为最⼤客⼾ —— ⽆论是前沿实验室还是超⼤规模云⼚商 —— 最为重
要的算⼒供应商之⼀。在 MI450 的量产爬坡上，我们在第四季度和 2027 年全年都⾼度专注；同

时我们也正与最⼤客⼾就 MI500 和 MI600 进⾏深度对话，着眼于赢得其更⼤⽐例的算⼒需求。从
我们所看到的⼀切来看，需求依然在持续上升。
关于企业市场，尽管企业客⼾的部署节奏确实⽐⼤型前沿实验室和超⼤规模云⼚商慢⼀些，但我
们认为企业市场⾮常重要。我们在企业 CPU ⽅⾯已取得了很⼤进展，许多企业客⼾正在主动咨
询我们关于 AI 部署效率的建议，包括本地部署和云端部署两个维度。我们正在扩展⾯向企业的产
品组合，推出了部分企业专属 GPU ，并希望在这⼀领域持续扩⼤份额，成为⼤型企业客⼾的战
略性核⼼供应商。
Harsh Kumar ：  好的，明⽩了。最后⼀个问题，关于客⼾业务：历史上您们在类似情况下管理
这⼀业务的能⼒有⽬共睹。如果主要竞争对⼿选择降价，贵公司的应对策略是什么？会跟进，还
是视情况⽽定？
Lisa Su ，董事⻓兼⾸席执⾏官：  Harsh ， PC 市场对我们来说是⼀个重要市场 —— 我始终相信端
到端 AI 部署的价值，从云端到边缘到客⼾端，我们拥有在这⼀维度上独特的完整产品组合。在此

基础上，我们在 PC 业务上的策略始终是⾼度聚焦于最⾼价值、最⾼盈利的细分市场 —— 企业、
⾼端消费、桌⾯渠道，这些都是我们的优势赛道。我们清醒地认识到低端市场存在较⼤波动，坦
率地说，那也是我们在 PC 市场中敞⼝最⼩的部分。
主持⼈结语
Matt Ramsay ，企业副总裁，财务战略与投资者关系：  ⾮常感谢 John 组织本次问答，也感谢各
位参与我们的电话会议，感谢⼤家的提问和关注，就由 John 来关闭本次会议。谢谢⼤家。
主持⼈：  感谢各位的参与。今天的电话会议到此结束，请各位挂断电话，祝⼤家有美好的⼀
天。

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -2.62 điểm tương ứng -0.15%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Công nghệ và Máy móc là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -13,987 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã HDB, PNJ và VJC; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-30 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,267 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -2,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 3,726 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -2,702 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua một tuần đi ngang giằng co, giảm nhẹ và đóng cửa ở 1.735 điểm 
với thanh khoản ở mức thấp, và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bán ròng cả 5 phiên trong tuần. 
Thông tin vĩ mô tích cực với tăng trưởng GDP quý 3 đạt mức gần 10% là điểm sáng trong 
tuần qua, tuy đã không đủ nâng đỡ thị trường chung. Thay vào đó, tâm lý thị trường thận 
trọng trước số liệu lạm phát ở mức cao, đợt tăng giá xăng dầu trong nước và mặt bằng lãi 
suất trong nước chưa có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt. Diễn biến tăng của giá dầu thế giới, và lợi suất 
trái phiếu Chính phủ Mỹ cũng là các yếu tố gây áp lực lên thị trường chung. Tâm lý thận 
trọng còn thể hiện qua áp lực bán giải chấp ở một số cổ phiếu riêng lẻ như PNJ, SHB..., dù 
một vài nhóm trụ như dầu khí, tiêu dùng có thời điểm đóng vai trò nâng đỡ chỉ số.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 đi ểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 đi ểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao phục 
vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức cao cho 
phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện đáng kể 
trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm phán vẫn 
đang bế tắc và ít có kh ả năng dòng ch ảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối lại ngay 
trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi l ựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đ ầu tư công, chính sách ti ền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

Xem đầy đủ tại đây.

Đỏ  Khả năng giảm điểm cao Bán mạnh

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành mẫu nến Doji trên khung đồ thị tuần sau hai tuần giảm điểm liên tiếp, 
cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại tại điểm đỡ gần quanh 1710 (đây cũng là chốt chặn cần 
giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn). 
Kháng cự xa: 1820 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1775 (+-10)

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
CONSUMER PANEL Q3 2026 
MORE RESILIENCE
3 November 2026
• Average consumer spend across 
the Westpac-DataX Consumer 
Panel remained resilient, rising 
1.5% in Q3 and 4.6%yr. 
• While some of this reflected a price-
driven surge in fuel spend (+0.3%qtr) 
discretionary categories also posted 
robust gains (contributing 0.9ppts 
to the headline rise) with non-fuel 
essential categories contributing 
positively as well (+0.5ptps).
• Households continued to add to savings 
and buffers. Savings rose 1.3%qtr, led 
by younger households and mortgagors, 
with average savings buffers increasing 
to 22.2 months of mortgage repayments. 
• The detail shows financial pressures 
remain uneven but spending patterns 
appear to be converging across 
consumers with low, medium and high 
buffers. Much of this appears to relate 
to a reduction in fuel consumption.

Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel
The Westpac–DataX Consumer Panel is a large 
dataset that gives a timely and detailed picture 
of Australian consumer finances and behaviour. 
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics 
team, the dataset links transaction activity with 
balance sheet information to give a complete view 
of income, spending, saving and borrowing flows. 
All data is de-identified and aggregated to 
ensure privacy. The resulting sample of over 
one million customers gives an accurate 
representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer and is perfectly suited 
to quickly tracing responses to events like 
interest rate moves and energy price shocks.

Fuel and non-fuel spending lift

### Page 2

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
Heatmap of monthly spending categories
The Westpac DataX Consumer Panel  shows average
consumer spending remained resilient in Q3, rising 1.5%
despite a further lift in fuel costs. Fuel contributed 0.3ppts
to quarterly growth, but discretionary spending did more

of the heavy lifting, contributing 0.9ppts as recreation
and travel-related spending recovered. Households also
continued to add to their financial buffers: savings up 1.3%
in the quarter and average buffers across households with
a mortgage increasing to 22.2 months of repayments.
Beneath the aggregate picture, lower-buffer households
remain more exposed to higher fuel costs and mortgage
repayments, but their discretionary spending share is
looking increasingly similar to other households.

Spending holds up despite higher fuel costs
Average household spending increased 1.5% in Q3, with much
of the strength again driven by transport-related spending.
Fuel contributed 0.3ppts to quarterly growth, while transport
excluding fuel contributed a further 0.4ppts. Excluding the
direct effects of the price-driven lift in fuel, average spend
was up 1.2%qtr. Importantly, stronger fuel spending did not
come at the expense of discretionary purchases. Discretionary

categories contributed 0.9ppts to quarterly spending growth,
compared with 0.5ppts from non-fuel essentials.
Solid rise in non-fuel spend and financial buffers
Discretionary spending dominates
Recreation and culture contributed 0.3ppts to growth, reflecting
stronger spending on sporting services, while restaurants and
hotels added a further 0.2ppts. Transport spending was also
boosted by a rebound in airline expenditure, which more than
unwound the weakness recorded in Q2 when refunds relating

to cancellations due to conflict in the Middle East weighed on
the category. Discretionary spending rose 0.9%qtr, following a
flat result and a 0.1% rise in the previous two quarters.
Illiana Jain
Economist, Westpac Group

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable,

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Savings growth by mortgage status
Households continue to rebuild savings
Savings increased 1.3% in Q3, with the strongest gains again 
recorded among younger households. Savings balances for 
18–34 year olds increased 2.0%qtr, compared with 0.7% 
for those aged 55 and over. Mortgagors also continued to 
accumulate savings more rapidly, recording a 2.8% increase 
compared with 0.7% among non-mortgagors. Savings growth 
among mortgagors has now outpaced non-mortgagors for 
three consecutive years.
Stronger savings have continued to lift mortgage buffers. 
Average buffers (savings divided by the sum of essential 
expenses and minimum mortgage repayments), increased to 
22.2 months of repayments in September. Looking within the 
unit-record sample, median mortgage buffers jumped from 
6.3 months in August to 6.8 months, its highest level since the 
series began in 2019.

Median mortgage buffers
Fuel spending a clear drag on consumers
Fuel spending as a share of total spending
Spending outcomes continued to vary across age groups. 
The squeeze remains most apparent among younger 
households. Fuel spending rose 5.1%qtr for 18–34 year olds, 
against a 1.5%qtr increase in non-fuel spending. By contrast, 
non-fuel spending increased 2.1%qtr among households aged 
55 and over. 
The gap between mortgagors and non-mortgagors was 
more modest. Spending rose 1.6%qtr among mortgagors 
and 1.9%qtr among those without a mortgage, suggesting 
mortgage status alone masks considerable variation in 
household financial positions – a divide that becomes clearer 
when households are grouped by the size of their mortgage 
buffers (see discussion further on in this note). Note that ‘non 
mortgagors’ covers a diverse range of consumers including 
those that are renting and those that own their homes without 
a mortgage.

### Page 4

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
4
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Median litres of fuel consumed
More importantly, their consumption appears to be less 
responsive to price movements. During periods of cheaper 
fuel, high-buffer households show a noticeably larger lift in 
litres purchased, consistent with stockpiling when prices are 
favourable. Low-buffer households show much less of this 
response, while their fuel volumes have also adjusted less 
during the recent increase in fuel costs.
The spending data show the consequence. Fuel expenditure 
has risen sharply across all three groups recently, but remains 
highest among low-buffer households, where it also absorbs 
a larger share of total spending. Food spending has also 
declined since 2023. This may partly reflect households 
economising within the category, including switching towards 
cheaper or home-brand products, although further work is 
needed to test this.
Taken together, the households with the smallest savings 
cushions face higher mortgage repayments, spend more 
on fuel and appear to have less scope to adjust their fuel 
consumption when prices change. 
Discretionary spend as a share of total
This suggests that in aggregate, household balance sheets 
are looking healthy. The picture is a little at odds with the very 
weak reads on our Westpac-Melbourne Index Consumer 
Sentiment Index which suggests finances are coming under 
much more pressure. The fact that both average spending and 
saving both rose suggests incomes over the quarter were not 
overly stretched and that more of the weakness in sentiment 
may relate to concerns about the outlook (something that may 
also be encouraging consumers to build buffers now, while they 
still can).
Pressures intense for low-buffer households
Aggregate buffer measures mask sizeable differences across 
mortgagors. We can get a sense of these variations by 
combining mortgagors into three broad groups according to 
buffer size, i.e. low, middle and high buffer groups. This shows 
that households with the smallest buffers are also more 
exposed to both interest rates and fuel costs.

The buffer differences are relatively stark, the median savings 
of the low-buffer group just $2k compared to $15k for the 
middle-buffer group and $95k for the high buffer group. 
Low buffer households face median minimum mortgage 
repayments of around $2,600 a month, compared with $2,400 
for the middle buffer group and $2,200 for the highest-buffer 
group. 
Low buffer households also spend more on fuel, at around 
$170 a month compared with $160 for the middle buffer 
group and $150 for the high buffer group.

We can use average pump prices to estimate what this 
expenditure implies for fuel volumes. Low-buffer households 
consume around 70 litres a month, middle-buffer households 
67 litres a month and the high-buffer group around 62 litres. 
This compares to an average of 81, 80 and 70 respectively 
through 2024-25. 
Median fuel spend by customer

### Page 5

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
Yet despite this greater exposure, the gap in broader spending 
behaviour has narrowed. High-buffer households continue 
to spend more on discretionary categories, averaging around 
$1,940 a month compared with $1,740 for low-buffer 
households. However, their discretionary share has been 
broadly stable since 2023, while the shares of low- and 
middle-buffer households have risen by close to a percentage 
point bringing them more in line with the high-buffer group.

Some of the convergence reflects a longer run down-trend in 
fuel spending that has been more pronounced amongst low 
and middle buffer households. This lowering in fuel spend lifts 
the share available for other categories.

Essential spending excluding fuel, meanwhile, has changed 
little across the three cohorts since 2023. The shift in 
spending patterns has largely occurred on the discretionary 
side.

The result is a somewhat mixed picture. Low-buffer 
households remain considerably more exposed to mortgage 
and fuel costs and have far smaller savings cushions. But their 
discretionary spending has nevertheless moved closer to that 
of better-buffered households, suggesting some recovery in 
spending flexibility despite these constraints. 
Conclusion
Aggregate household finances remain resilient: spending and 
savings both increased in Q3 and mortgage buffers continued 
to rebuild. Discretionary spending has also held up despite the 
sharp increase in fuel costs.

But aggregate resilience masks a much more uneven 
distribution of pressure. Low-buffer households 
simultaneously face higher mortgage repayments, greater fuel 
exposure and much smaller savings cushions. While these 
households have so far maintained discretionary spending, 
their capacity to absorb further pressure is clearly more 
limited.
About the Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel
Developed by DataX, Westpac’s data analytics service for 
institutional and business banking customers, the dataset links 
transaction activity with balance sheet information to give 
a complete view of income, spending, saving and borrowing 
flows. 
The resulting sample of over one million customers provides 
an accurate representation of trends across the wider 
Australian consumer. Monthly observations are available back 
to January 2019 with timely updates provided a few weeks 
after the end of each month. As such, it is perfectly suited to 
quickly tracing the impact and responses to events like interest 
rate moves and energy price shocks.

Document 403: Open mouth operations (2026-11-04)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Open mouth operations (2026-11-04).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-04
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
Open mouth operations
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
13:34 October 23 2026 Share
The RBA does not want to promise what it will do, but it is increasingly trying to tell us what
it thinks.
Central banks’ communication is carefully scrutinised for every nuance. Lately, there seems to have
been more RBA communication to pore over. The method of delivery has also evolved. This has

### Page 2

Market participants pore over every utterance by central banks for clues to future rate moves. They will
have plenty to pore over in the run-up to the November RBA decision. After the flurry of fireside chats
this week, ’60 Minutes’ will run a pre-recorded interview with chief economist Sarah Hunter on Sunday.
Next Wednesday, the whole senior policy team will appear before Senate Estimates, just as the
quarterly CPI is released. (This timing will have been the Senate’s choice, not the RBA’s.) After the

decision will come the Statement on Monetary Policy, a media release and press conference. More
speeches will follow, including one by the Deputy Governor on the Thursday.
RBA communication does seem to have changed, not just following the RBA Review but also more
recently. There may be fewer policy meetings each year, but the communication outside that certainly
feels more frequent than a couple of years ago. Back in 2024, speaking engagements felt a little

sparse outside the post-meeting media conference and parliamentary appearances. We have counted
58 public engagements so far this year on the Speeches page on the RBA’s website. This compares
with 58 for the whole of last year and 52 in 2024. Excluding the ‘compulsory’ press conferences and
parliamentary appearances, fewer than half of the 2024 events were related to economics, markets
and monetary policy.
The ‘how’ has changed
The way the RBA communicates with markets has changed as well. There are fewer set-piece

speeches on policy and a lot more semi-scripted ‘fireside chats’. Compared with a few years ago, there
are more interviews across a wide range of media. This is a big change from the pre-Review world. It
was a huge deal when the previous Governor appeared on the 7:30 Report; it is hard to imagine him
agreeing to an Assistant Governor appearing on 60 Minutes.
Part of the change reflects increased efforts to reach a general audience. Central banks want to explain

why they do what they do. This matters especially when interest rates are rising but the household
sector is not the source of the inflation.
Speaking differently to different audiences can sometimes confuse markets. As the RBA’s Chief
Communication Officer highlighted in a recent panel discussion, the post-meeting media conference is
designed to speak to the public. Market participants and economists certainly tune in, but we are not
sometimes caused confusion when tone seems to vary. Different audiences call for different tone and

varying complexity.
The content has evolved, too. RBA officials are increasingly using speeches directed to professional
audiences to discuss more technical estimates of inferred variables such as neutral rates and
financial conditions. They are also highlighting that monetary policy combats inflation in more ways
than just squeezing mortgage holders.
One thing has not changed: the RBA is not in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ promises

about the future path of interest rates. It does, however, give more running commentary about how it
is interpreting recent data. These interpretations are themselves useful signals about what the RBA
thinks it might do.

### Page 3

the primary audience. So if you think that what the Governor said in the media conference sounded
more ‘dovish’ than what another official says in a more market-oriented forum, it is partly because she
is trying to convey that the RBA understands that its recent policy actions are making things harder for
many Australians. But this is in much the same way that a doctor might say, “This might hurt a bit.” It
does, but that will not stop the treatment.

A related issue is that it is easier to control tone in pre-drafted material such as the minutes and set-
piece speeches than in unstructured Q&A. Senior central bankers are trained to be careful with their
words in unscripted settings. If the tone differs, the minutes are still more likely to say what the Board
really means than an off-the-cuff answer.
Greater communication with the broader public might have reduced the pressure to make the set-piece

speeches more accessible. Research on central bank communication often uses measures such as
the Flesch–Kincaid Grade Level and Flesch Reading Ease score. A comprehensive analysis was
beyond the scope of this note. However, a spot comparison suggests four speeches from 2026 (1, 2, 3,
4) were harder to read than four from 2021 (1, 2, 3, 4). The 2021 speeches scored in the 11 to 12
range, which is high-school-level complexity. The 2026 speeches scored between 12 and 15, which is

academic or technically dense text requiring university-level education. (Including two of my own 2021
speeches is an obvious caveat, but the two speeches by then-Governor Phil Lowe also scored better
on these metrics than the sample from this year. It should be noted, though, that readability metrics do
not necessarily imply better clarity if the concepts discussed are more abstract.)
The ‘what’ has changed
As noted above, the content of RBA communication has evolved, too. As its intellectual framework has

begun to put more weight on the balance of supply and demand, and those invisible ‘star’ variables
(NAIRUs, neutral real rates, ‘financial conditions’, etc.), the communication to professional audiences
has increasingly focused on those topics.
More recently, the RBA has also placed more emphasis on the full range of transmission channels from
interest rates to inflation (though not, as we have noted previously, the labour supply channel). This is

understandable in a world of supply shocks. People need to understand that monetary policy is not
simply an exercise in hitting mortgage borrowers. We doubt they would be comforted, though, by the
Deputy Governor’s explanation this week that the ‘cashflow channel’ was the smallest of the
transmission channels from interest rates to inflation, with the exchange rate much more prominent.
That implies a lot of pain for a small group, for little effect on inflation. It raises the question of whether

other policy tools or institutional arrangements might get the same effect on inflation through the other
channels without hitting a minority of households disproportionately.
We have also seen a move away from using catchphrases to summarise the position. From ‘narrow
path’ to ‘not ruling anything in or out’ to ‘cautious and predictable’, once entrenched, a phrase can
outlast its usefulness.

### Page 4

Related articles
One thing that has not changed is the way the minutes record the presentations to the Board and the
members’ discussion. The minutes rarely convey much new information beyond the post-meeting
statements. Sometimes, though, the minutes reveal the Board’s reaction to the staff’s analysis. The
August 2024 minutes were a good example: the discussion highlighted just how sensitive the forecasts
were to key judgements by the staff.
The ‘why’ has not changed

Despite these changes, the underlying objectives of the RBA’s communication have not changed. It
wants to explain – and perhaps justify – its decisions. And it wants to describe how it interprets
incoming data, especially inflation, the labour market and the balance of supply and demand.
The RBA, like any central bank, is not really in the business of providing ‘forward guidance’ about
future moves. Circumstances change, so it can never guarantee a particular future path for interest

rates. Some academic literature argues that promising to keep rates low for a long time (or high, for
that matter) independently amplifies the transmission of monetary policy beyond the effect of current-
period interest rates alone, by shifting the short end of the yield curve. Central banks including the RBA
have certainly tried jawboning in this way from time to time. The prevailing view, though, is that the
potential cost of reputational loss if things do not turn out as predicted outweighs the benefits of a

slightly stronger transmission mechanism.
In this context, it is the interpretation of data that provides the signal about future policy directions. This
is why it was noteworthy that the Governor still characterised the labour market as ‘a little tight’ after the
September meeting, even though the Board’s post-meeting communication did not. We have been
noting for some time that the RBA staff have a downbeat view of supply capacity. Together with the

RBA’s oft-stated concerns about inflation, this is why the Deputy Governor’s ‘hawkishness’ did not
surprise us.

Document 417: Rates Spark_ US midterms and expanding European government bond concerns

Tuesday will be nothing more than the day ahead of a much
bigger day – Wednesday, by which time we'll have guidance on
the midterm implications, and we'll have the US refunding
announcement. In Europe, political jitters in Belgium highlight
that France is not the only one struggling to consolidate its
ﬁnances

Padhraic Garvey, CFA and Benjamin Schroeder
Along with the results of the US midterm elections, the Treasury's refunding
operations loom large
The midterms and refunding loom large on
Wednesday
Two things for Treasuries to get their teeth into in the coming days.
First, the signals from the midterm elections. Expect this to be an
issue for trading on Wednesday, as projections emerge through
Tuesday night. The basic market discount is for the Democrats to
take the House, and maybe the Senate. The only big surprise would
be if the Republicans held on to both, which would be a huge
surprise, as it would, in fact, suggest an endorsement from the US

electorate for the war, tariﬀs and the current immigration policy,
among other contentious issues that have emboldened the left
and caused right-leaning independents to swing left. In all
probability, this would be bond negative, on the theory that it's
more of the same until disproven. The more probable blue sweep
should have little market impact, as it should be broadly
discounted.
The second big thing for Wednesday is the refunding
announcement. At the previous one, there was a clear intimation of

no change to the rhythm of issuance. And the questions posed to
primary dealers a few weeks back did not suggest anything big or
imminent in terms of change. However, there has been some
growing talk of the refunding including a reduction in long-end
issuance. More likely, if the Treasury were minded to do so, it would
be an indication that it has the ﬂexibility to do so going forward.
Either that, or no change to the issuance proﬁle, is probable. We

don't think the Treasury needs to panic here by changing tack on
issuance. Swap spreads are well-behaved. Better to leave such a
policy for a time when there is a material need to tighten swap
spreads.
European bond spreads will remain in focus
for some time, and it’s not just France
The European government bond space has also moved back into
focus at the start of the week, although France has fared better
than one might have feared. In an unscheduled review, Fitch

lowered the outlook on France’s A+ rating to negative, citing weaker
growth, higher borrowing costs and a wider deﬁcit since the August
review. Nonetheless, the 10y OAT/Bund spread still tightened by
around 4bp against the backdrop of a broader tightening in
government bond spreads at the start of the week, which seems to
have been driven by some easing of geopolitical tensions, with oil
prices coming oﬀ their highs. To be fair, France has been trading at

levels more akin to a BBB name for some time already, suggesting
spreads are already wider than pure fundamentals would imply.
